## Immigration and Refugee Protection Act {#immigration-and-refugee-protection-act .Title-of-Act}

[S.C.]{.abbr title="Statutes of Canada"} 2001, c. 27
:::

Assented to 2001-11-01

An Act respecting immigration to Canada and the granting of refugee
protection to persons who are displaced, persecuted or in danger
::::

::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: section
::: Enacts
Her Majesty, by and with the advice and consent of the Senate and House
of Commons of Canada, enacts as follows:
:::

## [Short Title]{.HTitleText1} {#h-274068 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Short title

**[[1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1 .sectionLabel}** This Act may be cited as
the [Immigration and Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5).

## [Interpretation]{.HTitleText1} {#h-274071 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  definitions in this subsection apply in this Act.

  [[Board]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Board]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Immigration and Refugee
      Board, which consists of the Refugee Protection Division, Refugee
      Appeal Division, Immigration Division and Immigration Appeal
      Division. ([Commission]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Convention Against Torture]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Convention Against Torture]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
      Convention Against Torture and Other Cruel, Inhuman or Degrading
      Treatment or Punishment, signed at New York on December 10, 1984.
      Article 1 of the Convention Against Torture is set out in the
      schedule. ([Convention contre la torture]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[designated foreign national]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[designated foreign national]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the
      meaning assigned by subsection 20.1(2). ([étranger
      désigné]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[foreign national]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[foreign national]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who is not
      a Canadian citizen or a permanent resident, and includes a
      stateless person. ([étranger]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[permanent resident]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[permanent resident]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who has
      acquired permanent resident status and has not subsequently lost
      that status under section 46. ([résident
      permanent]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Refugee Convention]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Refugee Convention]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the United
      Nations Convention Relating to the Status of Refugees, signed at
      Geneva on July 28, 1951, and the Protocol to that Convention,
      signed at New York on January 31, 1967. Sections E and F of
      Article 1 of the Refugee Convention are set out in the
      schedule. ([Convention sur les réfugiés]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Act includes regulations and
  instructions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Unless otherwise indicated, references in this Act to
  "this Act" include regulations made under it and instructions given
  under subsection 14.1(1).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 27, s. 2
- 2012, c. 17, s. 2, c. 19, s. 700
:::

Version](section-2-20120628.html "Link to previous version of section 2"){.PITLink}
:::

## [Objectives and Application]{.HTitleText1} {#h-274085 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Objectives --- immigration

- **[[3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  objectives of this Act with respect to immigration are

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to permit Canada to pursue the maximum social,
    cultural and economic benefits of immigration;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to enrich and strengthen the social and cultural
    fabric of Canadian society, while respecting the federal, bilingual
    and multicultural character of Canada;

  - [(b.1)]{.lawlabel} to support and assist the development of minority
    official languages communities in Canada;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} to support the development of a strong and
    prosperous Canadian economy, in which the benefits of immigration
    are shared across all regions of Canada;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} to see that families are reunited in Canada;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} to promote the successful integration of permanent
    residents into Canada, while recognizing that integration involves
    mutual obligations for new immigrants and Canadian society;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} to support, by means of consistent standards and
    prompt processing, the attainment of immigration goals established
    by the Government of Canada in consultation with the provinces;

  - [(f.1)]{.lawlabel} to maintain, through the establishment of fair
    and efficient procedures, the integrity of the Canadian immigration
    system;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} to facilitate the entry of visitors, students and
    temporary workers for purposes such as trade, commerce, tourism,
    international understanding and cultural, educational and scientific
    activities;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} to protect public health and safety and to maintain
    the security of Canadian society;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} to promote international justice and security by
    fostering respect for human rights and by denying access to Canadian
    territory to persons who are criminals or security risks; and

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} to work in cooperation with the provinces to secure
    better recognition of the foreign credentials of permanent residents
    and their more rapid integration into society.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Objectives --- refugees

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The objectives of this Act with respect to refugees
  are

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to recognize that the refugee program is in the
    first instance about saving lives and offering protection to the
    displaced and persecuted;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to fulfil Canada's international legal obligations
    with respect to refugees and affirm Canada's commitment to
    international efforts to provide assistance to those in need of
    resettlement;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} to grant, as a fundamental expression of Canada's
    humanitarian ideals, fair consideration to those who come to Canada
    claiming persecution;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} to offer safe haven to persons with a well-founded
    fear of persecution based on race, religion, nationality, political
    opinion or membership in a particular social group, as well as those
    at risk of torture or cruel and unusual treatment or punishment;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} to establish fair and efficient procedures that
    will maintain the integrity of the Canadian refugee protection
    system, while upholding Canada's respect for the human rights and
    fundamental freedoms of all human beings;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} to support the self-sufficiency and the social and
    economic well-being of refugees by facilitating reunification with
    their family members in Canada;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} to protect the health and safety of Canadians and
    to maintain the security of Canadian society; and

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} to promote international justice and security by
    denying access to Canadian territory to persons, including refugee
    claimants, who are security risks or serious criminals.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} This Act is to be construed and applied in a manner
  that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} furthers the domestic and international interests
    of Canada;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} promotes accountability and transparency by
    enhancing public awareness of immigration and refugee programs;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} facilitates cooperation between the Government of
    Canada, provincial governments, foreign states, international
    organizations and non-governmental organizations;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} ensures that decisions taken under this Act are
    consistent with the Canadian Charter of Rights and Freedoms,
    including its principles of equality and freedom from discrimination
    and of the equality of English and French as the official languages
    of Canada;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} supports the commitment of the Government of Canada
    to enhance the vitality of the English and French linguistic
    minority communities in Canada; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} complies with international human rights
    instruments to which Canada is signatory.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 27, s. 3
- 2012, c. 1, s. 205

## [Enabling Authority]{.HTitleText1} {#h-274120 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minister of Citizenship and Immigration

- **[[4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Except
  as otherwise provided in this section, the Minister of Citizenship and
  Immigration is responsible for the administration of this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Designated Minister

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may, by order, designate a
  minister of the Crown as the Minister responsible for all matters
  under this Act relating to special advocates. If none is designated,
  the Minister of Justice is responsible for those matters.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minister of Public Safety and Emergency
  Preparedness

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister of Public Safety and Emergency
  Preparedness is responsible for the administration of this Act as it
  relates to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} examinations at ports of entry;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the enforcement of this Act, including arrest,
    detention and removal;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the establishment of policies respecting the
    enforcement of this Act and inadmissibility on grounds of security,
    violating human or international rights, sanctions, transborder
    criminality or organized criminality; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} declarations referred to in section 42.1.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minister of Employment and Social
  Development

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} In making regulations under paragraphs 32(d.1) to
  (d.4), the Governor in Council may confer powers and duties on the
  Minister of Employment and Social Development.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Specification

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (1) to (2), the Governor in
  Council may, by order,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} specify which Minister referred to in any of
    subsections (1) to (2) is the Minister for the purposes of any
    provision of this Act; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} specify that more than one Minister may be the
    Minister for the purposes of any provision of this Act and specify
    the circumstances under which each Minister is the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Any order made under subsection (3) must be published
  in Part II of the [Canada Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 27, s. 4
- 2005, c. 38, s. 118

:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

- **[[5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Except
  as otherwise provided, the Governor in Council may make any regulation
  that is referred to in this Act or that prescribes any matter whose
  prescription is referred to in this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Regulations made under this Act that apply in
  respect of sponsorship applications or applications for permanent or
  temporary resident visas, permanent or temporary resident status or
  work or study permits may, if they so provide, apply in respect of any
  such applications that are pending on the day on which the regulations
  are made, other than

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} applications to become a permanent resident made in
    Canada by protected persons; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} applications for permanent resident visas made by
    persons referred to in subsection 99(2) and sponsorship applications
    made in respect of those applications.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tabling and referral of proposed
  regulations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall cause a copy of each proposed
  regulation made under sections 17, 32, 53, 61, 87.2, 102, 116, 150 and
  150.1 to be laid before each House of Parliament, and each House shall
  refer the proposed regulation to the appropriate Committee of that
  House.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Alteration of proposed regulation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A proposed regulation that has been laid before each
  House of Parliament under subsection (2) does not need to be so laid
  again, whether or not it has been altered.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Making of regulations

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make the regulation at
  any time after the proposed regulation has been laid before each House
  of Parliament under subsection (2).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 27, s. 5
- 2004, c. 15, s. 70
- 2008, c. 3, s. 2
- 2012, c. 19, s. 702
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-5-20080222.html "Link to previous version of section 5"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Designation of officers

- **[[6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister may designate any persons or class of persons as officers to
  carry out any purpose of any provision of this Act, and shall specify
  the powers and duties of the officers so designated.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delegation of powers

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Anything that may be done by the Minister under this
  Act may be done by a person that the Minister authorizes in writing,
  without proof of the authenticity of the authorization.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (2), the Minister may not delegate
  the power conferred by subsection 20.1(1), section 22.1 or subsection
  42.1(1) or (2) or 77(1).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 27, s. 6
- 2012, c. 17, s. 3
- 2013, c. 16, ss. 3, 36
:::

[Previous
Version](section-6-20120628.html "Link to previous version of section 6"){.PITLink}
:::

## [Agreements]{.HTitleText1} {#h-274166 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}International agreements

**[[7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7 .sectionLabel}** The Minister, with the
approval of the Governor in Council, may enter into an agreement with
the government of a foreign state or with an international organization
for the purposes of this Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Federal-provincial agreements

- **[[8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister, with the approval of the Governor in Council, may enter into
  an agreement with the government of any province for the purposes of
  this Act. The Minister must publish, once a year, a list of the
  federal-provincial agreements that are in force.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consistency with agreement

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3) but despite the other
  provisions of this Act, the following must be consistent with the
  federal-provincial agreements:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the selection and sponsorship of, and the
    acquisition of status by, foreign nationals under this Act; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} regulations governing those matters, including
    regulations respecting the examination in Canada of applications to
    become a permanent resident, or respecting the foreign nationals who
    may be selected on the basis of an investment in Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inadmissibility not limited

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (2) is not to be interpreted as limiting
  the application of any provision of this Act concerning
  inadmissibility to Canada.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sole provincial responsibility ---
permanent residents

- **[[9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where a
  province has, under a federal-provincial agreement, sole
  responsibility for the selection of a foreign national who intends to
  reside in that province as a permanent resident, the following
  provisions apply to that foreign national, unless the agreement
  provides otherwise:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the foreign national, unless inadmissible under
    this Act, shall be granted permanent resident status if the foreign
    national meets the province's selection criteria;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the foreign national shall not be granted permanent
    resident status if the foreign national does not meet the province's
    selection criteria;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the foreign national shall not be granted permanent
    resident status contrary to the provisions of the law of the
    province governing the number of foreign nationals who may settle in
    the province as permanent residents, whether that number is an
    estimate or a maximum, or governing the distribution of that number
    among classes of foreign nationals; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} conditions imposed in accordance with the law of
    the province have the same force and effect as if they were made
    under this Act, if they are imposed on a foreign national on or
    before the grant of permanent resident status.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sole provincial responsibility ---
  appeals

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a federal-provincial agreement gives a province
  sole responsibility to establish and apply financial criteria with
  respect to undertakings that sponsors living in that province may make
  in respect of a foreign national who applies to become a permanent
  resident, then, unless the agreement provides otherwise, the existence
  of a right of appeal under the law of that province respecting
  rejections by provincial officials of applications for sponsorship,
  for reasons of failing to meet financial criteria or failing to comply
  with a prior undertaking, prevents the sponsor, except on humanitarian
  and compassionate grounds, from appealing under this Act against a
  refusal, based on those reasons, of a visa or permanent resident
  status.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consultations with the provinces

- **[[10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister may consult with the governments of the provinces on
  immigration and refugee protection policies and programs, in order to
  facilitate cooperation and to take into consideration the effects that
  the implementation of this Act may have on the provinces.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Required consultations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must consult with the governments of the
  provinces respecting the number of foreign nationals in each class who
  will become permanent residents each year, their distribution in
  Canada taking into account regional economic and demographic
  requirements, and the measures to be undertaken to facilitate their
  integration into Canadian society.

## [PART 1]{.HLabel1}[Immigration to Canada]{.HTitleText1} {#h-274192 .Part}

### [DIVISION 0.01]{.HLabel2}[Biometric Information]{.HTitleText2} {#h-274193 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Biometric information

**[[10.01]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.01 .sectionLabel}** A person who makes
a claim, application or request under this Act must follow the
procedures set out in the regulations for the collection and
verification of biometric information, including procedures for the
collection of further biometric information for verification purposes
after a person's claim, application or request is allowed or accepted.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 36, s. 168
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

**[[10.02]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.02 .sectionLabel}** The regulations may
provide for any matter relating to the application of section 10.01 and
may include provisions respecting

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} restrictions on the persons to whom that section
  applies and on the claims, applications or requests to which it
  applies;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the procedures for the collection and verification of
  biometric information;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the biometric information that is to be collected;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the circumstances in which a person is not required
  to provide certain biometric information;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the processing of the collected biometric
  information, including the creation of biometric templates or the
  conversion of the information into digital biometric formats; and

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} the circumstances in which a person is exempt from
  the application of that section.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 36, s. 168
:::

### [DIVISION 0.1]{.HLabel2}[Invitation to Make an Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-274208 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for permanent residence ---
invitation to apply

- **[[10.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  foreign national who seeks to enter or remain in Canada as a member of
  a class that is referred to in an instruction given under paragraph
  10.3(1)(a) may make an application for permanent residence only if the
  Minister has issued them an invitation to do so, the invitation has
  not been cancelled under subsection 10.2(5) and the applicable period
  specified in an instruction given under paragraph 10.3(1)(k) has not
  expired.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Invitation to provincial nominees

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} A foreign national who is a member of a portion of
  the prescribed class of provincial nominees set out in an instruction
  given under paragraph 10.3(1)(a) may be issued an invitation only in
  respect of that class.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An instruction may be given under paragraph
  10.3(1)(a) only in respect of a class that is part of the economic
  class referred to in subsection 12(2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Provincial nominees

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} In the case of the prescribed class of provincial
  nominees, an instruction may be given under paragraph 10.3(1)(a) in
  respect of the foreign nationals who are nominated by the government
  of a particular province in accordance with an agreement referred to
  in section 8, or in respect of a portion of those foreign nationals.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Expression of interest

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A foreign national who wishes to be invited to make
  an application must submit an expression of interest to the Minister
  by means of an electronic system in accordance with instructions given
  under section 10.3 unless the instructions provide that they may do so
  by other means.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inadmissible foreign national

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A foreign national may not submit an expression of
  interest if they have been determined to be --- and continue to be ---
  inadmissible for misrepresentation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}New expression of interest

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A foreign national who has submitted an expression of
  interest may not submit another one before the expiry of the period
  referred to in an instruction given under paragraph 10.3(1)(f).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Failure to make application

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} A foreign national who is invited to make an
  application and does not do so within the period specified in an
  instruction given under paragraph 10.3(1)(k) is not eligible to be
  invited to make an application in relation to the expression of
  interest on the basis of which the invitation was issued.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Declining invitation

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (6) does not apply to a foreign national
  who declines an invitation to make an application within the period
  specified in an instruction given under paragraph 10.3(1)(k).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2013, c. 40, s. 290
- 2014, c. 20, s. 306
- 2017, c. 20, s. 300
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-10.1-20150101.html "Link to previous version of section 10.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Expression of interest --- processing

- **[[10.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  processing an expression of interest, the Minister

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is to determine whether the foreign national is
    eligible to be invited to make an application by applying the
    criteria set out in instructions given under paragraph 10.3(1)(e)
    and is to advise the foreign national of the determination in
    accordance with instructions given under paragraph 10.3(1)(l); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection (2), is to determine whether,
    in accordance with instructions given under paragraph 10.3(1)(i),
    the foreign national occupies the rank required to be invited to
    make an application and, if so, is to issue the invitation in
    accordance with instructions given under paragraph 10.3(1)(l).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A determination under paragraph (1)(b) may be made
  only if the number of invitations that have been issued is less than
  the number provided for in an instruction given under paragraph
  10.3(1)(j).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Electronic system

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister is to use an electronic system to carry
  out any applicable instruction given under subsection 10.3(1) and to
  make a determination under paragraph (1)(a) or (b).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compliance with instructions

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} An expression of interest must be processed in
  compliance with any applicable instruction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cancellation of invitation

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may cancel an invitation to make an
  application if the invitation was issued in error.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2013, c. 40, s. 290
- 2014, 20, s. 306
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Instructions

- **[[10.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.3
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may give instructions
  governing any matter relating to the application of this Division,
  including instructions respecting

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the classes in respect of which subsection 10.1(1)
    applies;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the electronic system referred to in subsections
    10.1(3) and 10.2(3);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the submission and processing of an expression of
    interest, including by means of the electronic system;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the circumstances in which an expression of
    interest may be submitted by means other than the electronic system
    and respecting those other means;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the criteria that a foreign national must meet to
    be eligible to be invited to make an application;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the period during which a foreign national remains
    eligible to be invited to make an application;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the personal information that the Minister may
    disclose under section 10.4 and the entities to which that
    information may be disclosed;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the basis on which an eligible foreign national may
    be ranked;

  - [(h.1)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection (1.01), the establishment
    of categories of eligible foreign nationals for the purposes of
    ranking, which groupings may consist of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} all eligible foreign nationals,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} eligible foreign nationals who are eligible to
      be members of a class referred to in an instruction given under
      paragraph (a), or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} eligible foreign nationals who are eligible to
      be members of a category established in an instruction given under
      paragraph (h.2);

  - [(h.2)]{.lawlabel} the establishment of categories for the purposes
    of ranking and the criteria for eligibility to be a member of a
    category;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the rank within a grouping that an eligible foreign
    national must occupy to be invited to make an application in respect
    of a class referred to in an instruction given under paragraph (a);

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} the number of invitations that may be issued within
    a specified period in respect of a grouping;

  - [(j.1)]{.lawlabel} the class referred to in an instruction given
    under paragraph (a) in respect of which an eligible foreign national
    who is invited to make an application must apply, if the foreign
    national is eligible to be a member of more than one class;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} the period within which an application must be made
    once an invitation has been issued;

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} the means by which a foreign national is to be
    advised of any matter relating to their expression of interest,
    including an invitation to make an application; and

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} any matter for which a recommendation to the
    Minister or a decision may or must be made by a designated person,
    institution or organization with respect to a foreign national.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Category --- condition

  [(1.01)]{.lawlabel} An instruction given under paragraph (1)(h.1) must
  not establish a category in respect of which a public consultation
  process referred to in subsection 10.5(1) has not been given the
  opportunity to provide advice and recommendations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Category --- economic goal

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister establishes a category in an
  instruction given under paragraph (1)(h.2), the Minister shall set
  out, in the instruction, the economic goal that the Minister seeks to
  support in establishing the category.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Clarification

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, an instruction given under
  paragraph (1)(j) may provide that the number of invitations that may
  be issued in any specified period in respect of a grouping be zero.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of instructions

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An instruction given under any of paragraphs
  (1)(a), (b) and (e) to (l) applies in respect of an expression of
  interest that is submitted before the day on which the instruction
  takes effect, unless the instruction provides otherwise.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Instructions given under subsection (1) must be
  published on the Department of Citizenship and Immigration's Internet
  site. Instructions given under any of paragraphs (1)(a), (d) to
  (g), (k) and (l) must also be published in the [Canada
  Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Criteria provided for under other
  Divisions

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, an instruction given under
  subsection (1) may provide for criteria that are more stringent than
  the criteria or requirements provided for in or under any other
  Division of this Act regarding applications for permanent residence.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2013, c. 40, s. 290
- 2014, c. 20, s. 306
- 2017, c. 20, s. 301
- [2022, c. 10, s. 377](#1357777-1367024){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1357777-1367024.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-10.3-20170622.html "Link to previous version of section 10.3"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure of information

**[[10.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.4 .sectionLabel}** For the purpose of
facilitating the selection of a foreign national as a member of the
economic class or as a temporary resident, the Minister may disclose to
an entity that is referred to in an instruction given under paragraph
10.3(1)(g) the personal information referred to in that instruction that
is

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} provided to the Minister by the foreign national
  under section 10.1 or by a third party for the purposes of section
  10.1 or 10.2; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} created by the Minister, on the basis of the
  information referred to in paragraph (a), for the purposes of sections
  10.1 to 10.3.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2013, c. 40, s. 290
- 2017, c. 20, s. 302
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-10.4-20150101.html "Link to previous version of section 10.4"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consultation process

- **[[10.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.5
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of establishing
  categories of eligible foreign nationals under subparagraph
  10.3(1)(h.1)(iii), the Minister must engage in a public consultation
  process with stakeholders, including provinces and territories,
  industry, unions, employers, workers, worker advocacy groups,
  settlement provider organizations and immigration researchers and
  practitioners, to obtain information, advice and recommendations in
  respect of the labour market conditions, including occupations
  expected to face shortage conditions, as well as on how categories can
  be formed to meet economic goals.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Advice and recommendations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The advice and recommendations from the public
  consultation process must be based on written submissions provided by
  relevant industry members and stakeholders.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall cause to be tabled before each
  House of Parliament, not later than the fifth sitting day of that
  House after January 31 following the end of each fiscal year, a report
  containing the list of the categories of eligible foreign nationals
  established in an instruction made under paragraph 10.3(1)(h.1) and
  the selection criteria and the process applied for the establishment
  of those categories.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Referral

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} After it is tabled, the report stands referred to the
  committee of the Senate, the House of Commons or both Houses of
  Parliament that may be designated or established for the purpose of
  reviewing the report.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 10, s. 377.1](#1357784-1367040){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1357784-1367040.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [DIVISION 1]{.HLabel2}[Requirements and Selection]{.HTitleText2} {#h-274277 .Subheading}

#### [Requirements]{.HTitleText3} {#h-274278 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application before entering Canada

- **[[11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  foreign national must, before entering Canada, apply to an officer for
  a visa or for any other document required by the regulations. The visa
  or document may be issued if, following an examination, the officer is
  satisfied that the foreign national is not inadmissible and meets the
  requirements of this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Electronic travel authorization

  [(1.01)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), a foreign national must,
  before entering Canada, apply for an electronic travel authorization
  required by the regulations by means of an electronic system, unless
  the regulations provide that the application may be made by other
  means. The application may be examined by an officer and, if the
  officer determines that the foreign national is not inadmissible and
  meets the requirements of this Act, the authorization may be issued by
  the officer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} A designated foreign national may not make an
  application for permanent residence under subsection (1)

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if they have made a claim for refugee protection
    but have not made an application for protection, until five years
    after the day on which a final determination in respect of the claim
    is made;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if they have made an application for protection,
    until five years after the day on which a final determination in
    respect of the application is made; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, until five years after the day
    on which they become a designated foreign national.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension of application

  [(1.2)]{.lawlabel} The processing of an application for permanent
  residence under subsection (1) of a foreign national who, after the
  application is made, becomes a designated foreign national is
  suspended

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the foreign national has made a claim for
    refugee protection but has not made an application for protection,
    until five years after the day on which a final determination in
    respect of the claim is made;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the foreign national has made an application for
    protection, until five years after the day on which a final
    determination in respect of the application is made; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, until five years after the day
    on which the foreign national becomes a designated foreign national.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refusal to consider application

  [(1.3)]{.lawlabel} The officer may refuse to consider an application
  for permanent residence made under subsection (1) if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the designated foreign national fails, without
    reasonable excuse, to comply with any condition imposed on them
    under subsection 58(4) or section 58.1 or any requirement imposed on
    them under section 98.1; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} less than 12 months have passed since the end of
    the applicable period referred to in subsection (1.1) or (1.2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}If sponsor does not meet requirements

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The officer may not issue a visa or other document to
  a foreign national whose sponsor does not meet the sponsorship
  requirements of this Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 27, s. 11
- 2008, c. 28, s. 116
- 2012, c. 17, s. 5, c. 31, s. 308
- 2015, c. 36, s. 169
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-11-20121214.html "Link to previous version of section 11"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[11.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.1 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 2015, c.
36, s. 170\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-11.1-20130429.html "Link to previous version of section 11.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Visa or other document not to be issued

- **[[11.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  officer may not issue a visa or other document in respect of an
  application for permanent residence to a foreign national who was
  issued an invitation under Division 0.1 to make that application if
  --- at the time the invitation was issued or at the time the officer
  received their application --- the foreign national

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} did not meet the criteria set out in an instruction
    given under paragraph 10.3(1)(e);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} did not have the qualifications on the basis of
    which they were ranked under an instruction given under paragraph
    10.3(1)(h) and were issued the invitation; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} did not meet the criteria for membership in a
    category that was established in an instruction given under
    paragraph 10.3(1)(h.2), if they were issued an invitation on the
    basis that they were eligible to be a member of that category.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), an officer may issue the visa
  or other document if, at the time the officer received their
  application,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the foreign national did not meet the criteria set
    out in an instruction given under paragraph 10.3(1)(e) --- or they
    did not have the qualifications on the basis of which they were
    ranked under an instruction given under paragraph 10.3(1)(h) or did
    not meet the criteria for membership in a category that was
    established in an instruction given under paragraph 10.3(1)(h.2) ---
    because the foreign national's birthday occurred after the
    invitation was issued; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the foreign national did not have the
    qualifications they had at the time the invitation was issued and on
    the basis of which they were ranked under an instruction given under
    paragraph 10.3(1)(h), but

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} they met the criteria set out in an instruction
      given under paragraph 10.3(1)(e),

    - [(i.1)]{.lawlabel} they met the criteria for membership of a
      category established in an instruction given under paragraph
      10.3(1)(h.2), if they were issued the invitation on the basis that
      they were eligible to be a member of that category, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} they occupied a rank that is not lower than the
      rank that a foreign national was required to have occupied to be
      invited to make an application.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 20, s. 300
- 2017, c. 20, s. 303
- [2022, c. 10, s. 378](#1357786-1367044){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1357786-1367044.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-11.2-20170622.html "Link to previous version of section 11.2"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Selection of Permanent Residents]{.HTitleText3} {#h-274314 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Family reunification

- **[[12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  foreign national may be selected as a member of the family class on
  the basis of their relationship as the spouse, common-law partner,
  child, parent or other prescribed family member of a Canadian citizen
  or permanent resident.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Economic immigration

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A foreign national may be selected as a member of the
  economic class on the basis of their ability to become economically
  established in Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refugees

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A foreign national, inside or outside Canada, may be
  selected as a person who under this Act is a Convention refugee or as
  a person in similar circumstances, taking into account Canada's
  humanitarian tradition with respect to the displaced and the
  persecuted.

#### [Sponsorship of Foreign Nationals]{.HTitleText3} {#h-274322 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sponsorship of foreign nationals

- **[[13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  Canadian citizen or permanent resident, or a group of Canadian
  citizens or permanent residents, a corporation incorporated under a
  law of Canada or of a province or an unincorporated organization or
  association under federal or provincial law --- or any combination of
  them --- may sponsor a foreign national, subject to the regulations.

- [(2) and (3)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2012, c. 17, s. 7\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Instructions of Minister

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} An officer shall apply the regulations on sponsorship
  referred to in paragraph 14(2)(e) in accordance with any instructions
  that the Minister may make.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 27, s. 13
- 2012, c. 17, s. 7
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-13-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 13"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Undertakings]{.HTitleText3} {#h-274331 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Undertaking binding

**[[13.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13.1 .sectionLabel}** An undertaking given
under this Act in respect of a foreign national --- including a
sponsorship undertaking --- is binding on the person who gives it.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2012, c. 17, s. 8
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Undertaking required

- **[[13.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13.2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  required to do so by the regulations, a foreign national who makes an
  application for a visa or for permanent or temporary resident status
  must obtain the undertaking specified in the regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minister's instructions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An officer must apply the regulations made under
  paragraph 14(2)(e.1) in accordance with any instructions that the
  Minister may give.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2012, c. 17, s. 8
:::

#### [Regulations]{.HTitleText3} {#h-274343 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

- **[[14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  regulations may provide for any matter relating to the application of
  this Division, and may define, for the purposes of this Act, the terms
  used in this Division.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The regulations may prescribe, and govern any matter
  relating to, classes of permanent residents or foreign nationals,
  including the classes referred to in section 12, and may include
  provisions respecting

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} selection criteria, the weight, if any, to be given
    to all or some of those criteria, the procedures to be followed in
    evaluating all or some of those criteria and the circumstances in
    which an officer may substitute for those criteria their evaluation
    of the likelihood of a foreign national's ability to become
    economically established in Canada;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} applications for visas and other documents and
    their issuance or refusal, with respect to foreign nationals and
    their family members;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the number of applications that may be processed or
    approved in a year, the number of visas and other documents that may
    be issued in a year, and the measures to be taken when that number
    is exceeded;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} conditions that may or must be imposed, varied or
    cancelled, individually or by class, on permanent residents and
    foreign nationals;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} sponsorships;

  - [(e.1)]{.lawlabel} undertakings, and penalties for failure to comply
    with undertakings;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} deposits or guarantees of the performance of
    obligations under this Act that are to be given by any person to the
    Minister;

  - [(f.1)]{.lawlabel} the power to inspect, including the power to
    require documents to be provided for inspection, for the purpose of
    verifying compliance with undertakings; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} any matter for which a recommendation to the
    Minister or a decision may or must be made by a designated person,
    institution or organization with respect to a foreign national or
    sponsor.

- [(3) to (5)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2015, c. 36, s. 171\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 27, s. 14
- 2012, c. 17, s. 9, c. 31, ss. 309, 313
- 2013, c. 16, s. 4
- 2014, c. 20, s. 301
- 2015, c. 36, s. 171
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-14-20171018.html "Link to previous version of section 14"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Minister's Instructions]{.HTitleText3} {#h-274361 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Economic immigration

- **[[14.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of supporting the
  attainment of economic goals established by the Government of Canada,
  the Minister may give instructions establishing a class of permanent
  residents as part of the economic class referred to in subsection
  12(2) and, in respect of the class that is established, governing any
  matter referred to in paragraphs 14(2)(a) to (g), 26(a), (b), (d)
  and (e) and 32(d) and the fees for processing applications for
  permanent resident visas or for permanent resident status and
  providing for cases in which those fees may be waived.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite any instruction given by the Minister under
  paragraph 87.3(3)(c), no more than 2,750 applications in a class
  established under subsection (1) may be processed in any year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of regulations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (4), regulations that apply to
  all classes prescribed under subsection 14(2) that are part of the
  economic class referred to in subsection 12(2) apply to a class
  established under subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may specify in an instruction that
  regulations made under subsection 14(2), paragraph 26(a), (b), (d)
  or (e) or 32(d) or subsection 89(1) do not apply to a class
  established under subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of regulations

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, regulations that apply to only
  one class prescribed under subsection 14(2) that is part of the
  economic class referred to in subsection 12(2) --- or to only certain
  classes that are part of that economic class --- do not apply to a
  class established under subsection (1). However, an instruction given
  under that subsection may specify otherwise.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of instructions

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The instructions do not apply in respect of a class
  prescribed by the regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compliance with instructions

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} An officer must comply with the instructions before
  processing an application and when processing one.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amendments to instructions

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} An instruction that amends another instruction may,
  if it so provides, apply in respect of applications in a class
  established by the original instruction that are pending on the day on
  which the amending instruction takes effect.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effective period --- limitation

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} An instruction given under subsection (1) has effect
  for the period specified in the instruction, which is not to exceed
  five years starting on the day on which the instruction first takes
  effect. No amendment to or renewal of an instruction may extend the
  five-year period.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Pending applications

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (9), the Minister may direct
  officers to process, after the end of the effective period of an
  instruction, applications in a class established by the instruction
  that were made during the period in which the instruction had effect.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}[Service Fees Act](/eng/acts/S-8.4)

  [(11)]{.lawlabel} The [Service Fees Act](/eng/acts/S-8.4) does not
  apply in respect of the fees referred to in subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication

  [(12)]{.lawlabel} Instructions must be published in the [Canada
  Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2012, c. 19, ss. 703, 710
- 2017, c. 20, s. 454
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-14.1-20130429.html "Link to previous version of section 14.1"){.PITLink}
:::

### [DIVISION 2]{.HLabel2}[Examination]{.HTitleText2} {#h-274389 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Examination by officer

- **[[15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  officer is authorized to proceed with an examination if a person makes
  an application to the officer in accordance with this Act or if an
  application is made under subsection 11(1.01).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Provincial criteria

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a foreign national referred to in
  subsection 9(1), an examination of whether the foreign national
  complies with the applicable selection criteria shall be conducted
  solely on the basis of documents delivered by the province indicating
  that the competent authority of the province is of the opinion that
  the foreign national complies with the province's selection criteria.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inspection

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An officer may board and inspect any means of
  transportation bringing persons to Canada, examine any person carried
  by that means of transportation and any record or document respecting
  that person, seize and remove the record or document to obtain copies
  or extracts and hold the means of transportation until the inspection
  and examination are completed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Instructions

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The officer shall conduct the examination in
  accordance with any instructions that the Minister may give.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 27, s. 15
- 2012, c. 31, s. 310
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-15-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 15"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Health insurance policy

**[[15.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15.1 .sectionLabel}** A health insurance
policy purchased from an insurance company outside Canada that is
approved by the Minister satisfies any requirement in an instruction
given under subsection 15(4) that a foreign national who applies for a
temporary resident visa in order to visit their Canadian citizen or
permanent resident child or grandchild for an extended period must have
private health insurance.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2023, c. 21, s. 2](#1412064-1414334){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1412064-1414334.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Obligation --- answer truthfully

- **[[16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person who makes an application must answer truthfully all questions
  put to them for the purpose of the examination and must produce a visa
  and all relevant evidence and documents that the officer reasonably
  requires.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Obligation --- appear for examination

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} A person who makes an application must, on request
  of an officer, appear for an examination.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Obligation --- relevant evidence

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a foreign national,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the relevant evidence referred to in subsection (1)
    includes photographic and fingerprint evidence; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} subject to the regulations, the foreign national
    must submit to a medical examination.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Obligation --- interview

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} A foreign national who makes an application must,
  on request of an officer, appear for an interview for the purpose of
  an investigation conducted by the Canadian Security Intelligence
  Service under section 15 of the [Canadian Security Intelligence
  Service Act](/eng/acts/C-23) for the purpose of providing advice or
  information to the Minister under section 14 of that Act and must
  answer truthfully all questions put to them during the interview.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Evidence relating to identity

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An officer may require or obtain from a permanent
  resident or a foreign national who is arrested, detained, subject to
  an examination or subject to a removal order, any evidence ---
  photographic, fingerprint or otherwise --- that may be used to
  establish their identity or compliance with this Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 27, s. 16
- 2010, c. 8, s. 2
- 2013, c. 16, s. 5
- 2015, c. 3, s. 108(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-16-20130619.html "Link to previous version of section 16"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

**[[17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-17 .sectionLabel}** The regulations may
provide for any matter relating to the application of this Division, and
may include provisions respecting the conduct of examinations.

### [DIVISION 3]{.HLabel2}[Entering and Remaining in Canada]{.HTitleText2} {#h-274418 .Subheading}

#### [Entering and Remaining]{.HTitleText3} {#h-274419 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Examination by officer

- **[[18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-18
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations, every
  person seeking to enter Canada must appear for an examination to
  determine whether that person has a right to enter Canada or is or may
  become authorized to enter and remain in Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transit

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) also applies to persons who, without
  leaving Canada, seek to leave an area at an airport that is reserved
  for passengers who are in transit or who are waiting to depart Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers of officer

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Even though a person seeking to enter Canada is not,
  in accordance with regulations made under subsection 26(2), required
  to appear for an examination, an officer may require the person to do
  so.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 27, s. 18
- 2017, c. 11, s. 5
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-18-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 18"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Right of entry of citizens and Indians

- **[[19]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-19 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every
  Canadian citizen within the meaning of the [Citizenship
  Act](/eng/acts/C-29) and every person registered as an Indian under
  the [Indian Act](/eng/acts/I-5) has the right to enter and remain in
  Canada in accordance with this Act, and an officer shall allow the
  person to enter Canada if satisfied following an examination on their
  entry that the person is a citizen or registered Indian.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Right of entry of permanent residents

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An officer shall allow a permanent resident to enter
  Canada if satisfied following an examination on their entry that they
  have that status.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Obligation on entry

- **[[20]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-20 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every
  foreign national, other than a foreign national referred to in section
  19, who seeks to enter or remain in Canada must establish,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to become a permanent resident, that they hold the
    visa or other document required under the regulations and have come
    to Canada in order to establish permanent residence; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to become a temporary resident, that they hold the
    visa or other document required under the regulations and will leave
    Canada by the end of the period authorized for their stay.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Declaration

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} A foreign national who is the subject of a
  declaration made under subsection 22.1(1) must not seek to enter or
  remain in Canada as a temporary resident.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Provincial criteria

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A foreign national referred to in subsection 9(1)
  must also establish, to become a permanent resident, that they hold a
  document issued by the province indicating that the competent
  authority of the province is of the opinion that the foreign national
  complies with the province's selection criteria.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 27, s. 20
- 2013, c. 16, s. 6
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-20-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 20"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Designation --- human smuggling or other
irregular arrival

- **[[20.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-20.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may, by order, having
  regard to the public interest, designate as an irregular arrival the
  arrival in Canada of a group of persons if he or she

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is of the opinion that examinations of the persons
    in the group, particularly for the purpose of establishing identity
    or determining inadmissibility --- and any investigations concerning
    persons in the group --- cannot be conducted in a timely manner; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} has reasonable grounds to suspect that, in relation
    to the arrival in Canada of the group, there has been, or will be, a
    contravention of subsection 117(1) for profit, or for the benefit
    of, at the direction of or in association with a criminal
    organization or terrorist group.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of designation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} When a designation is made under subsection (1), a
  foreign national --- other than a foreign national referred to in
  section 19 --- who is part of the group whose arrival is the subject
  of the designation becomes a designated foreign national unless, on
  arrival, they hold the visa or other document required under the
  regulations and, on examination, the officer is satisfied that they
  are not inadmissible.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}[Statutory Instruments
  Act](/eng/acts/S-22)

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An order made under subsection (1) is not a statutory
  instrument for the purposes of the [Statutory Instruments
  Act](/eng/acts/S-22). However, it must be published in the [Canada
  Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2012, c. 17, s. 10
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for permanent residence ---
restriction

- **[[20.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-20.2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  designated foreign national may not apply to become a permanent
  resident

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if they have made a claim for refugee protection
    but have not made an application for protection, until five years
    after the day on which a final determination in respect of the claim
    is made;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if they have made an application for protection,
    until five years after the day on which a final determination in
    respect of the application is made; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, until five years after the day
    on which they become a designated foreign national.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension of application for permanent
  residence

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The processing of an application for permanent
  residence of a foreign national who, after the application is made,
  becomes a designated foreign national is suspended

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the foreign national has made a claim for
    refugee protection but has not made an application for protection,
    until five years after the day on which a final determination in
    respect of the claim is made;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the foreign national has made an application for
    protection, until five years after the day on which a final
    determination in respect of the application is made; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, until five years after the day
    on which the foreign national becomes a designated foreign national.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refusal to consider application

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The officer may refuse to consider an application for
  permanent residence if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the designated foreign national fails, without
    reasonable excuse, to comply with any condition imposed on them
    under subsection 58(4) or section 58.1 or any requirement imposed on
    them under section 98.1; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} less than 12 months have passed since the end of
    the applicable period referred to in subsection (1) or (2).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2012, c. 17, s. 10
:::

#### [Status and Authorization to Enter]{.HTitleText3} {#h-274473 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Permanent resident

- **[[21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-21 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  foreign national becomes a permanent resident if an officer is
  satisfied that the foreign national has applied for that status, has
  met the obligations set out in paragraph 20(1)(a) and subsection 20(2)
  and is not inadmissible.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Protected person

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Except in the case of a person described in
  subsection 112(3) or a person who is a member of a prescribed class of
  persons, a person whose application for protection has been finally
  determined by the Board to be a Convention refugee or to be a person
  in need of protection, or a person whose application for protection
  has been allowed by the Minister, becomes, subject to any
  federal-provincial agreement referred to in subsection 9(1), a
  permanent resident if the officer is satisfied that they have made
  their application in accordance with the regulations and that they are
  not inadmissible on any ground referred to in section 34, 35 or 35.1,
  subsection 36(1) or section 37 or 38.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Pending application --- subsection
  108(2)

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A person in respect of whom the Minister has made an
  application under subsection 108(2) may not become a permanent
  resident under subsection (2) while the application is pending.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 27, s. 21
- 2012, c. 17, s. 11
- [2023, c. 19, s. 2](#1411896-1414294){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1411896-1414294.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-21-20120628.html "Link to previous version of section 21"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Temporary resident

- **[[22]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-22 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  foreign national becomes a temporary resident if an officer is
  satisfied that the foreign national has applied for that status, has
  met the obligations set out in paragraph 20(1)(b), is not inadmissible
  and is not the subject of a declaration made under subsection 22.1(1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Dual intent

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An intention by a foreign national to become a
  permanent resident does not preclude them from becoming a temporary
  resident if the officer is satisfied that they will leave Canada by
  the end of the period authorized for their stay.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 27, s. 22
- 2013, c. 16, s. 7
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-22-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 22"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Declaration

- **[[22.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-22.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may, on the Minister's
  own initiative, declare that a foreign national, other than a foreign
  national referred to in section 19, may not become a temporary
  resident if the Minister is of the opinion that it is justified by
  public policy considerations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effective period

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A declaration has effect for the period specified by
  the Minister, which is not to exceed 36 months.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may, at any time, revoke a declaration
  or shorten its effective period.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report to Parliament

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The report required under section 94 must include the
  number of declarations made under subsection (1) and set out the
  public policy considerations that led to the making of the
  declarations.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2013, c. 16, s. 8
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Entry to complete examination or hearing

**[[23]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-23 .sectionLabel}** An officer may authorize
a person to enter Canada for the purpose of further examination or an
admissibility hearing under this Part.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Temporary resident permit

- **[[24]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  foreign national who, in the opinion of an officer, is inadmissible or
  does not meet the requirements of this Act becomes a temporary
  resident if an officer is of the opinion that it is justified in the
  circumstances and issues a temporary resident permit, which may be
  cancelled at any time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A foreign national referred to in subsection (1) to
  whom an officer issues a temporary resident permit outside Canada does
  not become a temporary resident until they have been examined upon
  arrival in Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Instructions of Minister

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In applying subsection (1), the officer shall act in
  accordance with any instructions that the Minister may make.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction --- pending application for
  protection

  [(3.1)]{.lawlabel} A foreign national whose claim for refugee
  protection has been determined to be ineligible to be referred to the
  Refugee Protection Division may not request a temporary resident
  permit if they have made an application for protection to the Minister
  that is pending.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A foreign national whose claim for refugee protection
  has not been allowed may not request a temporary resident permit if
  less than 12 months have passed since

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the day on which their claim was rejected or
    determined to be withdrawn or abandoned by the Refugee Protection
    Division, in the case where no appeal was made and no application
    was made to the Federal Court for leave to commence an application
    for judicial review; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the latest of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the day on which their claim was rejected or
      determined to be withdrawn or abandoned by the Refugee Protection
      Division or, if there was more than one such rejection or
      determination, the day on which the last one occurred,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the day on which their claim was rejected or
      determined to be withdrawn or abandoned by the Refugee Appeal
      Division or, if there was more than one such rejection or
      determination, the day on which the last one occurred, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the day on which the Federal Court refused
      their application for leave to commence an application for
      judicial review, or denied their application for judicial review,
      with respect to their claim.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction --- designated foreign
  national

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A designated foreign national may not request a
  temporary resident permit

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if they have made a claim for refugee protection
    but have not made an application for protection, until five years
    after the day on which a final determination in respect of the claim
    is made;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if they have made an application for protection,
    until five years after the day on which a final determination in
    respect of the application is made; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, until five years after the day
    on which the foreign national becomes a designated foreign national.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension of request

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The processing of a request for a temporary resident
  permit of a foreign national who, after the request is made, becomes a
  designated foreign national is suspended

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the foreign national has made a claim for
    refugee protection but has not made an application for protection,
    until five years after the day on which a final determination in
    respect of the claim is made;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the foreign national has made an application for
    protection, until five years after the day on which a final
    determination in respect of the application is made; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, until five years after the day
    on which the foreign national becomes a designated foreign national.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refusal to consider request

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} The officer may refuse to consider a request for a
  temporary resident permit if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the designated foreign national fails, without
    reasonable excuse, to comply with any condition imposed on them
    under subsection 58(4) or section 58.1 or any requirement imposed on
    them under section 98.1; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} less than 12 months have passed since the end of
    the applicable period referred to in subsection (5) or (6).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 27, s. 24
- 2010, c. 8, s. 3
- 2012, c. 17, s. 12
- [2019, c. 29, s. 302](#1156657-1173916){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1156657-1173916.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-24-20120628.html "Link to previous version of section 24"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Humanitarian and compassionate
considerations --- request of foreign national

- **[[25]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-25
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (1.2), the
  Minister must, on request of a foreign national in Canada who applies
  for permanent resident status and who is inadmissible --- other than
  under section 34, 35, 35.1 or 37 --- or who does not meet the
  requirements of this Act, and may, on request of a foreign national
  outside Canada --- other than a foreign national who is inadmissible
  under section 34, 35, 35.1 or 37 --- who applies for a permanent
  resident visa, examine the circumstances concerning the foreign
  national and may grant the foreign national permanent resident status
  or an exemption from any applicable criteria or obligations of this
  Act if the Minister is of the opinion that it is justified by
  humanitarian and compassionate considerations relating to the foreign
  national, taking into account the best interests of a child directly
  affected.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction --- designated foreign
  national

  [(1.01)]{.lawlabel} A designated foreign national may not make a
  request under subsection (1)

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if they have made a claim for refugee protection
    but have not made an application for protection, until five years
    after the day on which a final determination in respect of the claim
    is made;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if they have made an application for protection,
    until five years after the day on which a final determination in
    respect of the application is made; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, until five years after the day
    on which they become a designated foreign national.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension of request

  [(1.02)]{.lawlabel} The processing of a request under subsection (1)
  of a foreign national who, after the request is made, becomes a
  designated foreign national is suspended

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the foreign national has made a claim for
    refugee protection but has not made an application for protection,
    until five years after the day on which a final determination in
    respect of the claim is made;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the foreign national has made an application for
    protection, until five years after the day on which a final
    determination in respect of the application is made; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, until five years after the day
    on which they become a designated foreign national.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refusal to consider request

  [(1.03)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may refuse to consider a request
  under subsection (1) if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the designated foreign national fails, without
    reasonable excuse, to comply with any condition imposed on them
    under subsection 58(4) or section 58.1 or any requirement imposed on
    them under section 98.1; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} less than 12 months have passed since the end of
    the applicable period referred to in subsection (1.01) or (1.02).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment of fees

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} The Minister is seized of a request referred to in
  subsection (1) only if the applicable fees in respect of that request
  have been paid.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptions

  [(1.2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may not examine the request if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the foreign national has already made such a
    request and the request is pending;

  - [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} the request is for an exemption from any of the
    criteria or obligations of Division 0.1;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the foreign national has made a claim for refugee
    protection that is pending before the Refugee Protection Division or
    the Refugee Appeal Division;

  - [(b.1)]{.lawlabel} the foreign national made a claim for refugee
    protection that was determined to be ineligible to be referred to
    the Refugee Protection Division and they made an application for
    protection to the Minister that is pending; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection (1.21), less than 12 months
    have passed since

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the day on which the foreign national's claim for
      refugee protection was rejected or determined to be withdrawn ---
      after substantive evidence was heard --- or abandoned by the
      Refugee Protection Division, in the case where no appeal was made
      and no application was made to the Federal Court for leave to
      commence an application for judicial review, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the latest of

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the day on which the foreign national's claim
        for refugee protection was rejected or determined to be
        withdrawn --- after substantive evidence was heard --- or
        abandoned by the Refugee Protection Division or, if there was
        more than one such rejection or determination, the day on which
        the last one occurred,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the day on which the foreign national's claim
        for refugee protection was rejected or determined to be
        withdrawn --- after substantive evidence was heard --- or
        abandoned by the Refugee Appeal Division or, if there was more
        than one such rejection or determination, the day on which the
        last one occurred, and

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} the day on which the Federal Court refused the
        foreign national's application for leave to commence an
        application for judicial review, or denied their application for
        judicial review, with respect to their claim for refugee
        protection.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception to paragraph (1.2)(c)

  [(1.21)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph (1.2)(c) does not apply in respect of a
  foreign national

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} who, in the case of removal, would be subjected to
    a risk to their life, caused by the inability of each of their
    countries of nationality or, if they do not have a country of
    nationality, their country of former habitual residence, to provide
    adequate health or medical care; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} whose removal would have an adverse effect on the
    best interests of a child directly affected.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of certain factors

  [(1.3)]{.lawlabel} In examining the request of a foreign national in
  Canada, the Minister may not consider the factors that are taken into
  account in the determination of whether a person is a Convention
  refugee under section 96 or a person in need of protection under
  subsection 97(1) but must consider elements related to the hardships
  that affect the foreign national.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Provincial criteria

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may not grant permanent resident status
  to a foreign national referred to in subsection 9(1) if the foreign
  national does not meet the province's selection criteria applicable to
  that foreign national.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 27, s. 25
- 2008, c. 28, s. 117
- 2010, c. 8, s. 4
- 2012, c. 17, s. 13
- 2013, c. 16, ss. 9, 36, c. 40, s. 291
- [2019, c. 29, s. 303](#1156664-1173925){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1156664-1173925.html"
  role="button"}
- [2023, c. 19, s. 3](#1411898-1414296){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1411898-1414296.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-25-20190621.html "Link to previous version of section 25"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Humanitarian and compassionate
considerations --- Minister's own initiative

- **[[25.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-25.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may, on the Minister's
  own initiative, examine the circumstances concerning a foreign
  national who is inadmissible --- other than under section 34, 35, 35.1
  or 37 --- or who does not meet the requirements of this Act and may
  grant the foreign national permanent resident status or an exemption
  from any applicable criteria or obligations of this Act if the
  Minister is of the opinion that it is justified by humanitarian and
  compassionate considerations relating to the foreign national, taking
